BUSINESS REVIEW                                                             業務回顧

In 2021, in a sophisticated world with challenges such as rising            2021年，在複雜的國際環境和疫情散發
COVID-19 cases, local governments across China diligently                   等多重危機的挑戰下，全國各地區認真
implemented decisions and arrangements by the CPC Central                   貫徹落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，科
Committee and the State Council, coordinated pandemic prevention            學統籌疫情防控和經濟社會發展，消費
and control and economic and social development. With these                 市場總體保持恢復態勢。但2021年調味
efforts, the overall consumer market continued its steady recovery.         品行業受需求疲軟、庫存積壓、成本上
However, the condiment industry in 2021 was affected by weakened            漲及社區團購分流等因素影響，整體呈
demand, excess inventory, rising costs and diversion of customers in        緩慢恢復的態勢。截至2021年12月31日
communities by group purchases. As a result, the industry maintained        止年度，本集團收入達到人民幣5,942.6
a slow recovery trend in general. For the year ended 31 December            百萬元，同比增長10.9%；淨利潤達到
2021, the revenue of the Group was RMB5,942.6 million, representing         人民幣857.6百萬元，同比降低12.7%。
a year-on-year increase of 10.9%; net profit was RMB857.6 million,
representing a year-on-year decrease of 12.7%.

     Foreign Exchange Risk and Hedging                                         匯率波動風險及相關對沖

     The Group mainly operates in the PRC with most of the transactions        本集團主要在中國經營，大部分交易乃
     denominated and settled in RMB. However, the Group has certain cash       以人民幣列值及結算。然而，本集團持
     in hand denominated in Hong Kong dollars and United States dollars,       有若干以港元及美元列值的現金，面臨
     and is therefore exposed to foreign exchange risks. The Group has         外匯匯兌風險。本集團並無對沖外匯風
     not hedged against its foreign exchange risks. However, the Group         險。然而，本集團將密切監控有關情況
     will closely monitor the exposure and will take specific measures when    並於必要時採取一定措施，確保外匯風
     necessary to make sure the foreign exchange risks are manageable          險處於可控範圍。

     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES OF THE COMPANY                             本公司的企業管治常規

     The Group is committed to achieving high standards of corporate           本集團致力達致高水準的企業管治，務
     governance to safeguard the interests of the Shareholders and to          求保障股東權益及提高企業價值、問責
     enhance corporate value, accountability and transparency.                 性及透明度。

     The Company has applied the principles as set out in the Corporate        本公司已應用上市規則附錄十四所載企
     Governance Code and also the corporate governance report as               業管治守則的原則及企業管治報告。
     contained in Appendix 14 to the Listing Rules.

     The Corporate Governance Code sets out the principles of good             企業管治守則訂明的良好企業管治原則
     corporate governance and two levels of corporate governance               及兩個層面的企業管治常規如下：
     practices, as follows:

     (a)   code provisions, which listed issuers are expected to comply with   (a)   守則條文，期望上市發行人遵守或
           or to give considered reasons for deviation; and                          就偏離行為提供審慎考慮的理由；
                                                                                     及

     (b)   recommended best practices for guidance only, which listed          (b)   建議最佳常規，僅屬指引，鼓勵上
           issuers are encouraged to comply with.                                    市發行人遵守。

     The Company ’ s corporate governance practices are based on               本公司的企業管治常規乃以企業管治守
     the principles and the code provisions as set out in the Corporate        則所載原則及守則條文為依據。
     Governance Code.

     The Company regularly reviews its compliance with Corporate               本公司定期審閱遵守企業管治守則的情
     Governance Code and the Company was in compliance with the code           況且本公司於截至2021年12月31日止年
     provisions of the Corporate Governance Code during the year ended         度內已遵守企業管治守則的守則條文。
     31 December 2021.

A.   THE BOARD                                                                A.   董事會

     1.   Responsibilities                                                         1.   責任

          The Board is responsible for the leadership and control of                    董事會負責領導和控制本公司
          the Company and is responsible for promoting the success                      及負責統籌及監督本公司的事
          of the Company by directing and supervising its affairs. The                  務進而促使本公司達致成功。
          Board also reviews the policies and practices on compliance                   董事會亦審閱遵守法律及法規
          with legal and regulatory requirements. The Board has                         規定方面的政策及常規。董事
          established Board committees and has delegated to these                       會下設董事委員會，並向該等
          Board committees’ various responsibilities as set out in their                董事委員會轉授其各自職權範
          respective terms of reference which are published on the                      圍載列的各項責任。該等董事
          websites of the Stock Exchange and the Company.                               委員會各自的職權範圍刊載於
                                                                                        聯交所及本公司網站。

          All of the Directors have carried out their duties in good faith              全體董事已真誠地依據適用法
          and in compliance with the standards of applicable laws and                   律及規例的標準履行職責，並
          regulations, and have acted objectively in the best interests                 客觀地為本公司及其股東的最
          of the Company and its Shareholders.                                          佳利益行事。

     2.   Delegation of Management Function                                        2.   管理層職能授權

          The Board is responsible for making all major decisions                       董事會負責制定本公司所有重
          of the Company including: the approval and monitoring                         大決策，包括：批准及監督本

e.   Principal Risk and Uncertainties                                          e.   主要風險及不確定性

     Risk of Raw Materials and Cost Control                                         原材料成本管控風險

     The global pandemic is still ongoing and there are occasionally                全球疫情仍在持續，局部疫情時有
     partial outbreaks. The condition of international trade is still severe        發生，國際貿易形勢依舊嚴峻，大
     with the rise in costs and large fluctuations in prices of bulk raw            宗原材料成本上漲及價格波動較
     materials. The rise in raw material costs would result in the drop in          大，原材料成本的上升會導致公司
     the gross profit margin of the Company.                                        毛利率水平的下降。

     Response Measures                                                              應對措施

     Our bulk purchase committee will lock in on the price and                      我們的大宗採購委員將結合原材料
     quality of raw materials with suppliers based on both the market               的行情以及年度銷售計劃，提前和
     condition and the annual sales plan to better stabilize the costs of           供應商鎖價鎖量，以更好地穩定產
     products. When rise in prices is predicted, we stock up from the               品的成本；當預判可能出現價格上
     supply system in advance, in order to lower the pressure of rising             漲時，提前與供應體系進行庫存備
     costs as a result of increased price of raw materials. We are also             貨，降低原材料價格上漲帶來的成
     considering further optimization of raw materials, for example,                本上升壓力；我們也在考慮原料的
     switching different categories of raw materials and seeking raw                進一步優化，比如原材料不同品種
     materials with better cost performance. In addition, we delegate               的切換，尋找性價比更優的原材
     the authority of bulk purchase to category committee members                   料。另外，我們將大宗採購權限下
     and the head of factory and include costs in their remuneration                放到品類委員及工廠長，並將成本
     review. At the same time, we have also formulated the micro-                   納入其薪酬考核體系，同時，也制
     innovation incentive management measures of the supply                         定了供應體系的微創新激勵管理辦
     systems, incentivizing the improvement of supply techniques and                法，激勵供應端工藝精進及成本效
     the maximization of cost effectiveness.                                        益的最大化。

     Risk of Food Safety                                                            食品安全風險

     The quality and safety of our products are very important to                   產品的質量及安全對我們持續經營
     our continuous operation. If our quality control system is not                 至關重要，一旦我們的質量控制體
     sufficiently implemented, quality or food safety issues may arise.             系出現控制不到位，則有可能面臨
     With a growing number of new products and the increase of the                  質量或食品安全問題，且隨着我們
     number of our self-owned factories and OEM factories, if the                   新產品的不斷增多，自有工廠及供
     management and control is poor, there will be food safety risks                應商數量增加，管控不足會存在食
     and product quality risks, which will have a material adverse                  品安全風險和產品質量風險，從而
     impact on the Company ’ s brand, reputation and financial                      對公司的品牌、聲譽及財務狀況造
     condition.                                                                     成重大不利影響。

      THE RESTRICTED SHARE UNIT SCHEME                                         受限制股份單位計劃

      The Company has approved and adopted a RSU Scheme by a                   本公司已根據股東於2016年2月24日的

ESG MANAGEMENT                                                             ESG管理方針

ESG Concept                                                                ESG理念

We believe that sustainable and robust ESG performance is essential        我們相信持續穩健的ESG表現對本集團
to the sustainable development of the Group ’ s business and the           業務與本集團所在社區的可持續發展至
community where the Group operates. The dynamic combination of             關重要，商業和社會需求的有機結合方
commercial and social needs is bound to embrace more influential           能產生更具影響的社會效應。我們不僅
social effects. We are not only committed to achieving sound financial     致力於實現強健的財務業績，亦致力於
performance, but also realising greater overall benefits through           積極識別各利益相關方的期望，不斷發
actively identifying stakeholders ’ expectations, discovering areas        掘自身業務發展和環境、社會需求的契
where our business development coheres with environmental and              合點，審慎管理自身的ESG風險，充
social demands, exercising prudent management over our ESG risks,          分整合現有及潛在資源以為社會創造更
fully integrating our existing and potential resources to provide more     具價值的產品和服務，努力提升企業價
value-added products and services for the society, striving to enhance     值，確保本集團的長期持續發展。
our corporate value and ensuring the Group’s long-term sustainable
development.

(commonly known as cadelle) in some warehouses that will enter the           鋸谷盜（俗稱米蟲）會通過包裝紙箱進入
outside of the instant fan box through the packaging carton. This year,      到方便粉絲盒子外部的情況。本年度，
we further strengthened the sealing of storage cartons to prevent such       我們進一步加強了對存儲紙箱的密封處
incidents.                                                                   理，杜絕了此類事件的發生。

      passed the certification can be introduced into use. After this, different   提前進行風險評估，並進行現場審核認
      OEM factories are managed in different ways based on their risk levels,      證，通過認證的公司方能引入使用；引

      Enhancement of the Supplier Daily Management                                夯實供應商體系建設

      Based on different raw materials, the risk levels and the Group’s special   本集團依據原料的種類、風險特性及本
      requirements, we have developed customised review standards and             集團對原料的特殊要求制定了定制化審
      specifications to better identify risks. The Group classifies suppliers     核標準規範，以便更好地區別風險。我
      into three levels of high-level, middle-level, and low-level based on       們根據原料使用量及風險級別將供應商
      quantity and risk levels of raw materials. High-level suppliers are the     分為高風險、中風險、低風險三級，其
      most important subject to strictest inspection, including salad oil, beef   中高風險供應商最重要，檢查也最為嚴
      tallow, tomato, Sichuan pepper, mushroom and so on. The Group has           格，包括色拉油、牛油、番茄、花椒、
      continuously strengthened the management of overseas and domestic           菌類等。本集團不斷強化海外和國內的
      suppliers, added national risk sampling concerns in the audit standards,    供應商管理，在審核標準中增加國家風
      as well as matters prone to complaints during the use of products,          險抽檢關注事項，以及在產品使用過程
      and clarified the control requirements. Based on different domestic         中容易產生投訴的事項，並明確管控要

      STEADY AND SUSTAINABLE DEVELOPMENT BASED ON                                合規為基，穩健持續發展
      COMPLIANCE

      Safeguard on Customers’ Rights and Interests                               維護客戶權益

      For the sake of active response to customers’ feedback, complaints          為了能夠積極應對消費者反饋投訴意見
      and suggestions, and protect consumer rights, the Group strictly            及建議，保護客戶權益，本集團嚴格遵
      abides by the Law of the People’s Republic of China on the Protection       守《中華人民共和國消費者權益保護法》
      of Consumer Rights and Interests , Product Quality Law of The Peoples      《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 產 品 質 量 法 》等 法 律
      Republic of China and other laws and regulations and develops relevant      法規，並在此基礎上制定了相關規章制
      rules and regulations on this basis. In 2021, the Group developed Yihai     度。本年度，我們制定了《頤海銷售渠道
      Sales Channel Management System , which specified standards and             管理制度》，補充了關於渠道及平台銷售
      specific procedures for channel and platform sales authorisation, and       授權的標準和具體流程，同時明確了對

          Governance Structure:                                                                 Board Statement
          A statement from the board containing the following elements:
          (i)   a disclosure of the board’s oversight of ESG issues;
          (ii) the board ’ s ESG management approach and strategy, including the
                process used to evaluate, prioritise and manage material ESG-related
                issues (including risks to the issuer’s businesses); and
          (iii) how the board reviews progress made against ESG-related goals and
                targets with an explanation of how they relate to the issuer’s businesses.
          管治架構：                                                                                 董事會聲明
          由董事會發出的聲明，當中載有下列內容：
          (i)   披露董事會對環境、社會及管治事宜的監管；
          (ii) 董事會的環境、社會及管治管理方針及策略，包括評估、優次排列及管理重
                要的環境、社會及管治相關事宜（包括對發行人業務的風險）的過程；及
          (iii) 董事會如何按環境、社會及管治相關目標檢討進度、並解釋它們如何與發行
                人業務有關連。

          致性）。

Key Audit Matter
關鍵審計事項

Impairment provision for inventories
存貨減值準備

Refer to Notes 2.12, 4.1(a) and 11 to the consolidated      請參閱綜合財務報表附註2.12, 4.1(a)及11
financial statements

Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and            存貨乃按其成本與可變現淨值（即為預估的銷售價格減去
net realisable value, being estimated selling price         預估的銷售成本）孰低者列賬，適用時，確認減值準備以
less estimated selling costs, where applicable, and         將存貨撇減至其可變現淨值。
impairment provision is recognised to write down the
inventories to their net realisable value.

As at 31 December 2021, the carrying amount of              於 2021 年 12 月 31 日， 貴集團存貨的賬面值為人民幣
the Group ’ s inventories was RMB395,280,000 and            395,280,000元及並無確認存貨減值準備。
no provision for impairment of inventories has been
recognised.

The estimation of impairment provision for inventories      存貨的減值準備估計涉及管理層重大判斷及估計，該等判
involves significant management ’ s judgment and            斷及估計需考慮庫齡結構、過往經驗、預估未來售價以及
estimates based on the consideration of certain             銷售開支等若干關鍵因素。
key factors such as aging profile, prior experiences,
estimated future selling prices and selling expenses.

The impairment assessment of inventories is an area of      鑑於存貨可變現淨值的估計受限於估計高度不確定性以及
focus for us given the estimation of net realisable value   存貨結額金額龐大，存貨減值評估是我們關注的範疇。
of inventories is subject to high degree of estimation
uncertainty and the significant amount of the inventory
balance.

      How our audit addressed the Key Audit Matter
      我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項

      Impairment provision for inventories
      存貨減值準備

      Our audit procedures relating to impairment provision       我們針對存貨減值準備的審計程式包括以下各項：
      for inventories included the following:

      •   We obtained and understood the Group’s inventory        •   我們獲得並了解了貴集團的存貨準備政策。
          provision policy.

      •   We understood and evaluated the relevant                •   我們對管理層就存貨可變現淨值採取的相關內部監控
          management’s internal controls and assessment               及評估程序進行了解及評估，並通過考慮估計的不確
          process of net realisable value of inventories and          定程度及其他固有風險因素（例如複雜性、主觀性、
          assessed the inherent risk of material misstatement         變動及受管理層偏見或欺詐影響的程度）水平，評估
          by considering the degree of estimation uncertainty         重大錯誤陳述的固有風險。
          and level of other inherent risk factors such as
          complexity, subjectivity, changes and susceptibility
          to management bias or fraud.

      •   We evaluated, on sample basis, the assumptions in       •   我們參考歷史記錄及年結日後的銷售交易，在抽樣的
          estimating future selling prices and selling expenses       基礎上對預估未來售價及銷售開支的假設進行了評
          by reference to historical records and sales                估。
          transactions after year end.

      •   We tested, on sample basis, the aging profile of the    •   在抽樣的基礎上，我們對年結日的原材料及產成品的
          year end raw materials and finished goods.                  庫齡結構進行了測試。

      •   We observed physical condition of inventories           •   我們在存貨盤點期間查看了存貨的物理狀況，以確定
          during stocktake to identify if any inventories were        存貨是否出現滯銷、損壞或報廢。
          slow-moving, damaged, or obsolete.

      Based on the above, we considered that management’s         基於上述各項，我們認為管理層在評估存貨減值準備時所
      judgement and estimates applied in the assessment of        作出的判斷及估計有可得的證據及所進行的程序支持。
      impairment provision for inventories were supportable
      by the evidence obtained and procedures performed.

      AUDITOR ’ S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE AUDIT OF THE                           核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責任
      CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS

      Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the         我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是

      report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a     內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔任何責
      guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with ISAs will always       任。合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能

            2.5 Segment reporting                                                        2.5 分部報告

                  Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent                   經營分部的呈報方式與向主要
                  with the internal reporting provided to the chief operating              經營決策者作出內部呈報的方

                  is responsible for allocating resources and assessing                    負責分配資源及評估經營分部
                  performance of the operating segments, has been identified               的表現，並已被認定為本公司
                  as the management of the Company (“Management”) that                     作出戰略決策的管理層（「管理
                  makes strategic decisions.                                               層」）。

            2.6 Foreign currency translation                                             2.6 外幣換算

                  (i)    Functional and presentation currency                              (i)    功能及呈報貨幣

     2.17 Current and deferred income tax                                           2.17 即期及遞延所得稅

          The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax                  本期間的所得稅開支或抵免指
          payable on the current period’s taxable income based on                     就本期間應課稅收入按各司法
          the applicable income tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted               權區的適用所得稅稅率支付的
          by changes in deferred income tax assets and liabilities                    稅項（就暫時差額及未動用稅
          attributable to temporary differences and to unused tax                     項虧損應佔的遞延所得稅資產
          losses.                                                                     及負債變動作出調整）。

          Current income tax                                                          即期所得稅

          The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of                 即期所得稅支出根據本公司及
          the tax laws enacted or substantively enacted at the end of                 其附屬公司營運及產生應課稅
          the reporting period in the countries where the Company                     收入所在的國家於報告期末已
          and its subsidiaries operate and generate taxable income.                   頒佈或實質已頒佈的稅法計
          Management periodically evaluates positions taken in                        算。管理層就適用稅務法規須
          tax returns with respect to situations in which applicable                  作出詮釋的情況定期評估報稅
          tax regulation is subject to interpretation. It establishes                 表的狀況，並須在適用情況下
          provisions where appropriate on the basis of amounts                        根據預期向稅務機關支付的稅
          expected to be paid to the tax authorities.                                 款計提撥備。

          Deferred income tax                                                         遞延所得稅

          Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the liability                遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產
          method, on temporary differences arising between the tax                    及負債的稅基與其在綜合財務
          bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying amounts in               報表中的賬面值之間的暫時差
          the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred                    額計提全額撥備。然而，倘遞
          income tax liabilities are not recognised if they arise from the            延所得稅負債於初步確認商譽
          initial recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is also not            時產生，則不予確認；倘自初
          accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset             步確認一項交易（業務合併除
          or liability in a transaction other than a business combination             外）中的資產或負債而產生的
          that at the time of the transaction affects neither accounting              遞延所得稅於交易時對會計或
          nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred income tax is determined               應課稅損益並不構成影響，則
          using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or                        該遞延所得稅亦不會列賬。遞
          substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period and                延所得稅乃使用於報告期末已
          are expected to apply when the related deferred income tax                  頒佈或實質已頒佈且預期將於
          asset is realised or the deferred income tax liability is settled.          相關遞延所得稅資產變現或遞

                  adjustment to equity.                                                      及對權益作出的相應調整。

     2.22 Leases                                                                2.22 租賃

          Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                      在本集團預期可使用租賃資產
          corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset            之日，租賃應確認為使用權資
          is available for use by the Group.                                       產和相應負債。

          Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                           合約可包含租賃及非租賃部
          components. The Group allocates the consideration in the                 分。本集團根據其相對獨立的
          contract to the lease and non-lease components based on                  價格將合約的代價分配至租賃
          their relative stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real           及非租賃部分。然而，就本集
          estate for which the Group is a lessee, it has elected not               團作為承租人租賃房地產而
          to separate lease and non-lease components and instead                   言，其已選擇不區分租賃及非
          accounts for these as a single lease component.                          租賃部分，而是將該等租賃入
                                                                                   賬作為單一租賃部分。

          Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and                    租賃條款按個別基準協商，並
          contain a wide range of different terms and conditions. The              包含多種不同的條款和條件。
          lease agreements do not impose any covenants other than                  除出租人持有租賃資產的抵押
          the security interests in the leased assets that are held by             權益外，租賃協議不施加任何
          the lessor. Leased assets may not be used as security for                契諾。租賃資產不得用作借款
          borrowing purposes.                                                      抵押。

          Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially                租賃產生的資產和負債在現值
          measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include             基礎上進行初始計量。租賃負
          the net present value of the following lease payments:                   債包括下列租賃付款額的淨現

          •    fixed payments (including in-substance fixed                        •      固定付款額（包括實質固
               payments), less any lease incentives receivable;                           定付款額），扣除應收的
                                                                                          租賃激勵；

          •    variable lease payment that are based on an index or a              •      取決於指數或比率的可
               rate;                                                                      變租賃付款額；

          •    amounts expected to be payable by the Group under                   •      本集團根據餘值擔保預
               residual value guarantees;                                                 計應付的金額；

          •    the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is             •      購買選擇權的行權價，
               reasonably certain to exercise that option; and                            前提是本集團合理確定
                                                                                          將行使該選擇權；及

          •    payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the             •      終止租賃的罰款金額，
               lease term reflects the Group exercising that option.                      前提是租賃期反映出本
                                                                                          集團將行使終止租賃選
                                                                                          擇權。

                         received by the individual lessee as a starting point,                  別承租人最近獲得的第
                         adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions                     三方融資為出發點作出

                  If a readily observable amortising loan rate is available to the           倘個別承租人（通過近期融資

                  which has a similar payment profile to the lease, then the                 攤銷貸款利率，而該等承租人

     2.23 Dividends distribution                                                 2.23 股息分派

          Provision is made for the amount of any dividend declared,                就於報告期末或之前已宣派但
          being appropriately authorised and no longer at the                       於報告期末並未分派的任何股

          period but not distributed at the end of the reporting period.            由實體酌情決定）作出撥備。

     2.24 Government grants                                                      2.24 政府補助

          Grants from the government are recognised at their fair                   當能夠合理地保證補助將可收
          value where there is a reasonable assurance that the grant                取及本集團將符合所有附帶條
          will be received and the Group will comply with all attached              件時，按公平值確認政府補
          conditions.                                                               助。

          Government grants relating to costs are deferred and                      與成本有關的政府補助遞延入
          recognised in profit or loss over the period necessary                    賬，並於需要匹配擬補償的相
          to match them with the costs that they are intended to                    關成本期間在損益確認。
          compensate.

          Government grants relating to the purchase of property,                   與購買物業、廠房及設備有關
          plant and equipment are included in non-current liabilities               的政府補助列入非流動負債作
          as deferred income and are credited to profit or loss on a                為遞延收入，並於有關資產的
          straight-line basis over the expected lives of the related                預計年期以直線法計入損益。
          assets.

     2.25 Interest income                                                        2.25 利息收入

          Interest income on financial assets at amortised cost                     採用實際利率法計算的按攤銷
          calculated using the effective interest method is recognised              成本計量的金融資產之利息收
          in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                 入，作為其他收入及收益淨額
          comprehensive income as part of other income and                          的一部分於綜合損益及其他全
          gains-net.                                                                面收益表確認。

          Interest income is presented as finance income where                      利息收入呈列為持作現金管理
          it is earned from financial assets that are held for cash                 用途的金融資產所賺取的融資
          management purposes, see Note 28 below. Any other                         收入，請參閱下文附註 28。

            2.25 Interest income (Continued)                                               2.25 利息收入（續）

                   Interest income is calculated by applying the effective                       利息收入是用實際利率乘以金
                   interest rate to the gross carrying amount of a financial                     融資產賬面總額計算得出，惟
                   asset except for financial assets that subsequently become                    後續發生信貸減值的金融資產
                   credit-impaired. For credit-impaired financial assets the                     除外。就信貸減值的金融資產
                   effective interest rate is applied to the net carrying amount of              而言，其利息收入是用實際利
                   the financial assets (after deduction of the loss allowance).                 率乘以金融資產賬面淨額（經
                                                                                                 扣除虧損撥備）得出。

                       Other financial assets at amortised cost                                   按攤銷成本計量的
                                                                                                  其他金融資產

                       Other financial assets at amortised cost mainly                             按攤銷成本計量的
                       include deposits for utilities and leases and other                         其他金融資產主要
                       receivables. The management of the Group                                    包括公用事業存
                       makes periodic collective assessments as well                               款、租賃及其他應
                       as individual assessment on the recoverability of                           收款項。本集團管
                       other receivables based on historical settlement                            理層根據過往結算
                       records and past experiences. The Group                                     記錄及過往經驗，
                       measures credit risk using Probability of Default                           定期對其他應收款
                       ( “ PD ” ), Exposure at Default ( “ EAD ” ) and Loss                        項的可收回性進行

      4.    CRITICAL ESTIMATES AND JUDGEMENTS                                     4.   重大估計及判斷

            The preparation of financial statements requires the use of                編製財務報表需要使用會計估計，
            accounting estimates which, by definition, will seldom equal the           顧名思義，該等估計甚少符合實際
            actual results. Management also needs to exercise judgement in             結果。管理層亦須在應用本集團會
            applying the Group’s accounting policies.                                  計政策時作出判斷。

            Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated. They are               估計及判斷會予以持續評估，且基
            based on historical experience and other factors, including                於歷史經驗及其他因素，包括對未
            expectations of future events that may have a financial impact             來事件的預期，而該等事件可能對
            on the entity and that are believed to be reasonable under the             實體造成財務影響且據信在某些情
            circumstances.                                                             況下屬合理。

            4.1 Critical accounting estimates                                          4.1 重大會計估計

                  (a)    Net realisable value of inventories                             (a)   存貨的可變現淨值

                         Net realisable value of inventories is the estimated                  存貨的可變現淨值按日
                         selling price in the ordinary course of business, less                常業務過程中的估計售
                         estimated costs of completion and selling expenses.                   價減估計完工成本及銷
                         These estimates are based on the current market                       售開支計算。該等估計
                         condition and historical experience of manufacturing                  乃基於當前市況以及製
                         and selling products of similar nature. Management                    造及銷售類似性質產品
                         reassesses these estimates at each balance sheet                      的過往經驗作出。管理
                         date.                                                                 層於各資產負債表日期
                                                                                               重估該等估計。

             accounted for                                                               理

             The Group leases various offices, warehouses and has                        本集團租賃多處辦公室、倉庫
             land use rights under long-term lease agreements. Lease                     及擁有長期租賃協議下的土地
             terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain                     使用權。每份合同的租賃條款
             a wide range of different terms and conditions. The lease                   均單獨商定，並且包括眾多不
             agreements do not impose any covenants, but leased assets                   同的條款及條件。租賃協議不
             may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                         包含契約條款，但租賃資產不
                                                                                         得用作借款抵押。

     (v)     Extension and termination options                                   (v)     續租及終止選擇權

             Extension and termination options are included in some                      續租及終止選擇權包含在本集
             property leases across the Group. These terms are used                      團若干物業租賃中。該等條款
             to maximise operational flexibility in terms of managing                    用於提高管理合約的運作靈活
             contracts. The majority of extension and termination options                性。所持有的大部分續租及終
             held are exercisable only by the Group and not by the                       止選擇權僅可由本集團行使，
             respective lessor.                                                          惟不可由各出租人行使。

20. RESTRICTED SHARE UNIT SCHEME                                         20. 受限制股份單位計劃

   The Company adopted Restricted Share Unit (the “RSU”) Scheme            本公司於2016年2月24日採納受限
   on 24 February 2016 with a duration of 10 years commencing              制股份單位（「受限制股份單位」）
   from the adoption date. The objective of the RSU Scheme is              計劃，自採納日期起計為期10年。
   to encourage and retain selected participants which include             受限制股份單位計劃的目的為鼓勵
   directors, employees, officers, and distributors of the Group, to       及留住特定參與者（包括本集團董
   work with the Group and to provide additional incentive for them        事、僱員、高級職員及經銷商）於
   to achieve performance goals.                                           本集團工作，並為彼等提供額外獎
                                                                           勵以實現業績目標。

   Under the RSU Scheme, the Company repurchased 53,680,000                根據受限制股份單位計劃，於2016
   ordinary shares from JLJH YIHAI Ltd., one of the shareholders of        年，本公司自本公司其中一名股東
   the Company, at a total consideration of US$638,108 (equivalent         JLJH YIHAI Ltd.購回53,680,000
   to RMB4,166,000) in 2016. The shares under the RSU Scheme               股普通股，總代價為638,108美元
   have been changed to 77,220,000 after the capitalisation issue          （相等於人民幣4,166,000 元）。於
   on 13 July 2016. These RSU shares have been held by Vistra              2016 年 7 月 13 日資本化發行後，
   Fiduciary (HK) Limited (“RSU Trustee”) in trust for the benefit of      受限制股份單位計劃中的股份已變
   the participants to the scheme and will be released to participants     更為 77,220,000 股。該等受限制
   upon vesting of each RSU.                                               股份單位股份以信託方式由Vistra
                                                                           Fiduciary (HK) Limited（「受限制股
                                                                           份單位受託人」）就計劃參與者的利
                                                                           益持有，並將於每個受限制股份單
                                                                           位歸屬後發放予參與者。

   The maximum number of ordinary shares that may be awarded by            董事會根據股份獎勵計劃可能獲得
   the Board of Directors pursuant to the Share Award Scheme must          的普通股數目上限合共不得超過本
   not, on an aggregate basis, exceed 10% of the issued ordinary           公司股本於2016年2月24日已發行
   shares in the share capital of the Company as at 24 February            普通股的10%。
   2016.

   The fair value of Restricted Shares based on the market value of        按本公司股份於授出日期的市場價
   the Company’s shares on the grant date is charged to profit or          值計算的受限制股份的公平值於本
   loss as employee benefit expenses of the Group.                         集團損益內作為僱員福利開支扣除。

   The Group has granted an aggregate of 10,652,000 shares under           本集團根據股份獎勵計劃授出合共
   the Share Award Scheme and all awarded shares were vested               10,652,000股股份，所有獎勵股份

34. RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                           34. 關聯方交易

   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability,       如一方有能力直接或間接控制另一
   directly or indirectly, to control the other party or exercise           方或對另一方作出財務及經營決策
   significant influence over the other party in making financial           行使重大影響力，則各方被視為有
   and operation decisions. Parties are also considered to be               關聯。如彼等受共同控制，各方亦
   related if they are subject to common control. Members of key            被視為有關聯。本集團主要管理層
   management and their close family member of the Group are also           成員及其近親亦被視為關聯方。
   considered as related parties.

   Save as disclosed elsewhere in these consolidated financial              除該等綜合財務報表其他部分所披
   statements, the Group has the following related party transactions,      露者外，本集團有以下關聯方交
   and they were carried out in the ordinary course of the Group’s          易，該等交易於本集團日常業務過
   business and were determined based on mutually agreed terms.             程中進行且基於相互協定的條款釐
                                                                            定。
